//! Private original-owner function entry, return and terminal pin retirement.
//! No instruction dispatcher, public invocation or initialized lifecycle exists.
#![allow(dead_code, clippy::result_large_err)]
use super::*;
use std::convert::Infallible;
use std::ops::ControlFlow;

#[derive(Clone, Copy, Debug, PartialEq, Eq)]
pub(crate) enum FrameDisposition {
    Idle,
    Active,
    Draining,
    Retained,
    RestorePending,
}
#[derive(Clone, Copy, Debug)]
pub(crate) struct FrameBound {
    pub previous: usize,
    pub admitted: usize,
}
pub(crate) struct FrameCleanupState {
    pub disposition: FrameDisposition,
    pub bound: Option<FrameBound>,
    pub unvisited: usize,
    pub nested_restore: Option<usize>,
    pub live_unreconciled: bool,
    pub entry_attempted: bool,
    pub entry: Option<EntryGuard>,
}
impl Default for FrameCleanupState {
    fn default() -> Self {
        Self::empty()
    }
}
impl FrameCleanupState {
    pub(crate) fn is_empty(&self) -> bool {
        self.disposition == FrameDisposition::Idle
            && self.bound.is_none()
            && self.unvisited == 0
            && self.nested_restore.is_none()
            && !self.live_unreconciled
            && !self.entry_attempted
            && self.entry.is_none()
    }
    pub(crate) const fn empty() -> Self {
        Self {
            disposition: FrameDisposition::Idle,
            bound: None,
            unvisited: 0,
            nested_restore: None,
            live_unreconciled: false,
            entry_attempted: false,
            entry: None,
        }
    }
}
#[must_use = "The actual State owns every pin; the recorded envelope remains until explicit completion"]
pub(crate) struct FrameOperationPhase<'a> {
    phase: ExecutionOperationPhase<'a>,
    cleanup: &'a mut FrameCleanupState,
    lifecycle: &'a mut Lifecycle,
}
pub(crate) struct PinAttempt {
    pub pin: Option<SwiftFunctionPin>,
    pub failure: Option<SwiftRunFailure>,
    pub nested_restore: Option<usize>,
}
#[derive(Clone, Copy, Debug)]
pub(crate) struct FrameScalars {
    pub function: u32,
    pub pc: usize,
    pub base: usize,
    pub operands: usize,
    pub controls: usize,
    pub result: bool,
}
#[derive(Clone, Copy, Debug)]
pub(crate) enum FrameReturn {
    CallerContinues,
    Outermost(Option<Value>),
}
#[derive(Clone, Copy, Debug)]
pub(crate) struct FrameDrainReport {
    pub retained_frames: usize,
    pub retained_entry: bool,
    pub live_unreconciled: bool,
    pub live: SwiftLiveUsage,
    pub cache: super::super::cache::SwiftCacheUsage,
    pub usage: SwiftRunUsage,
    pub bound: Option<FrameBound>,
    pub nested_restore: Option<usize>,
    pub first_failure: Option<SwiftRunFailure>,
}
#[derive(Clone, Copy, Debug)]
pub(crate) enum FrameCompletion {
    Completed,
    Retained(FrameDrainReport),
    RestoreRejected(FrameDrainReport),
}
/// Exact fixed caller storage. No generic T at this boundary.
pub(crate) struct FrameOperationStorage<'a> {
    pub preparation: Option<Result<FrameOperationPhase<'a>, SwiftRunFailure>>,
    pub entry: Option<Result<(), SwiftRunFailure>>,
    pub returned: Option<Result<FrameReturn, SwiftRunFailure>>,
    pub drained: Option<FrameDrainReport>,
}

/// This helper performs cleanup-independent provenance and layout checks only.
/// Neither resumed cleanup nor ticket validation checks fuel/cancellation/work.
pub(super) fn original_owners(
    control: &mut RuntimeControl,
    cache: &SwiftFunctionCache,
    wasi: &AccountedWasi,
    state: &SwiftExecutionState,
    cursor: Cursor,
) -> Result<(), SwiftRunFailure> {
    wasi.checked_usage(control, cursor)?;
    cache.check_owner(control, Some(wasi), cursor)?;
    let header = match state.execution_header.as_ref() {
        Some(header) => header,
        None => return Err(layout_failure(control, wasi, cursor)),
    };
    control.check_reservation_owner(header, cursor, Some(wasi.usage()))?;
    if !header.matches_layout(
        SwiftReservationCategory::State,
        execution_header_bytes().unwrap_or(usize::MAX),
    ) {
        return Err(layout_failure(control, wasi, cursor));
    }
    let requests = match arena_bytes(
        state.values.capacity(),
        state.controls.capacity(),
        state.frames.capacity(),
        state.memory.capacity() / PAGE,
        state.table.capacity(),
        state.globals.capacity(),
    ) {
        Some(requests) => requests,
        None => return Err(layout_failure(control, wasi, cursor)),
    };
    let budget = control.budget();
    if state.values.capacity() != budget.values
        || state.controls.capacity() != budget.controls
        || state.frames.capacity() != budget.frames
        || state.memory.len() != state.memory.capacity()
        || state.memory.len() % PAGE != 0
        || state.table.len() != state.table.capacity()
        || state.globals.len() != state.globals.capacity()
        || control.usage(Some(wasi.usage())).live.memory_pages.current != state.memory.len() / PAGE
    {
        return Err(layout_failure(control, wasi, cursor));
    }
    for (index, ticket) in state.buffers.iter().enumerate() {
        let ticket = match ticket.as_ref() {
            Some(ticket) => ticket,
            None => return Err(layout_failure(control, wasi, cursor)),
        };
        control.check_reservation_owner(ticket, cursor, Some(wasi.usage()))?;
        if !ticket.matches_layout(SwiftReservationCategory::State, requests[index]) {
            return Err(layout_failure(control, wasi, cursor));
        }
    }
    Ok(())
}
impl SwiftReactorInstance {
    pub(crate) fn prepare_frames(
        &mut self,
        cursor: Cursor,
    ) -> Result<FrameOperationPhase<'_>, SwiftRunFailure> {
        let Self {
            control,
            cache,
            wasi,
            state,
            lifecycle,
        } = self;
        let control = control.as_mut().expect("constructed control");
        let cache = cache.as_mut().expect("constructed cache");
        let wasi = wasi.as_mut().expect("constructed WASI");
        let state = state.as_mut().expect("constructed State");
        original_owners(control, cache, wasi, state, cursor)?;
        let live = control.usage(Some(wasi.usage())).live;
        if matches!(lifecycle, Lifecycle::Terminal)
            || !state.frame_cleanup.is_empty()
            || !state.frames.is_empty()
            || !state.controls.is_empty()
            || !state.values.is_empty()
            || cache.usage().active_pins != 0
            || live.values.current != 0
            || live.controls.current != 0
            || live.frames.current != 0
            || live.active_pins.current != 0
        {
            return Err(construction_failure(
                control,
                wasi,
                cursor,
                SwiftRunFailureKind::Invariant,
                "swift_frame_prepare_nonempty",
            ));
        }
        let previous = control
            .usage(Some(wasi.usage()))
            .reservations
            .construction
            .current;
        let admitted = match FRAME_OPERATION_BYTES.and_then(|bytes| previous.checked_add(bytes)) {
            Some(admitted) => admitted,
            None => return Err(layout_failure(control, wasi, cursor)),
        };
        control.enlarge_construction_with_wasi(admitted, cursor, Some(wasi.usage()))?;
        // No fallible boundary exists after admission and before its State record.
        state.frame_cleanup.bound = Some(FrameBound { previous, admitted });
        state.frame_cleanup.disposition = FrameDisposition::Active;
        Ok(FrameOperationPhase {
            phase: ExecutionOperationPhase {
                parts: ExecutionParts {
                    cache,
                    memory: &mut state.memory,
                    table: &mut state.table,
                    globals: &mut state.globals,
                    values: &mut state.values,
                    controls: &mut state.controls,
                    frames: &mut state.frames,
                },
                operation: SwiftExecutionOperation {
                    control,
                    wasi,
                    cursor,
                },
                previous_bound: previous,
            },
            cleanup: &mut state.frame_cleanup,
            lifecycle,
        })
    }
    pub(crate) fn resume_frame_cleanup(
        &mut self,
    ) -> Result<FrameOperationPhase<'_>, SwiftRunFailure> {
        let cursor = cleanup_cursor();
        let Self {
            control,
            cache,
            wasi,
            state,
            lifecycle,
        } = self;
        let control = control.as_mut().expect("constructed control");
        let cache = cache.as_mut().expect("constructed cache");
        let wasi = wasi.as_mut().expect("constructed WASI");
        let state = state.as_mut().expect("constructed State");
        original_owners(control, cache, wasi, state, cursor)?;
        let bound = match state.frame_cleanup.bound {
            Some(bound) => bound,
            None => return Err(layout_failure(control, wasi, cursor)),
        };
        if state.frame_cleanup.disposition == FrameDisposition::Idle
            || FRAME_OPERATION_BYTES.and_then(|bytes| bound.previous.checked_add(bytes))
                != Some(bound.admitted)
            || control
                .usage(Some(wasi.usage()))
                .reservations
                .construction
                .current
                < bound.admitted
        {
            return Err(layout_failure(control, wasi, cursor));
        }
        // An explicit reacquisition starts a new terminal pass. Retained frames
        // are only indexed pin storage and never supply live execution offsets.
        *lifecycle = Lifecycle::Terminal;
        state.frame_cleanup.disposition = FrameDisposition::Draining;
        state.frame_cleanup.unvisited = state.frames.len();
        state.frame_cleanup.entry_attempted = false;
        Ok(FrameOperationPhase {
            phase: ExecutionOperationPhase {
                parts: ExecutionParts {
                    cache,
                    memory: &mut state.memory,
                    table: &mut state.table,
                    globals: &mut state.globals,
                    values: &mut state.values,
                    controls: &mut state.controls,
                    frames: &mut state.frames,
                },
                operation: SwiftExecutionOperation {
                    control,
                    wasi,
                    cursor,
                },
                previous_bound: bound.previous,
            },
            cleanup: &mut state.frame_cleanup,
            lifecycle,
        })
    }
}
impl FrameOperationPhase<'_> {
    fn invariant(&mut self, code: &'static str) -> SwiftRunFailure {
        self.phase.operation.control.fail(
            self.phase.operation.cursor,
            Fault::new(SwiftRunFailureKind::Invariant, code),
            Some(self.phase.operation.wasi.usage()),
        )
    }
    fn limit(
        &mut self,
        kind: SwiftRunFailureKind,
        resource: SwiftRunResource,
        code: &'static str,
        attempted: usize,
        limit: usize,
    ) -> SwiftRunFailure {
        let mut fault = Fault::new(kind, code);
        fault.resource = Some(resource);
        fault.attempted = attempted as u64;
        fault.limit = limit as u64;
        self.phase.operation.control.fail(
            self.phase.operation.cursor,
            fault,
            Some(self.phase.operation.wasi.usage()),
        )
    }
    fn terminal(&mut self) {
        *self.lifecycle = Lifecycle::Terminal;
        if self.cleanup.disposition == FrameDisposition::Active {
            self.cleanup.disposition = FrameDisposition::Draining;
            self.cleanup.unvisited = self.phase.parts.frames.len();
        }
    }
    fn active(&mut self) -> Result<(), SwiftRunFailure> {
        if self.cleanup.disposition != FrameDisposition::Active
            || matches!(self.lifecycle, Lifecycle::Terminal)
            || self.cleanup.entry.is_some()
            || self.cleanup.nested_restore.is_some()
            || self.cleanup.live_unreconciled
        {
            return Err(self.invariant("swift_frame_inactive"));
        }
        self.agreement()
    }
    fn agreement(&mut self) -> Result<(), SwiftRunFailure> {
        let live = self
            .phase
            .operation
            .control
            .usage(Some(self.phase.operation.wasi.usage()))
            .live;
        if live.values.current != self.phase.parts.values.len()
            || live.controls.current != self.phase.parts.controls.len()
            || live.frames.current != self.phase.parts.frames.len()
            || live.active_pins.current != self.phase.parts.cache.usage().active_pins
            || live.active_pins.current != self.phase.parts.frames.len()
        {
            self.cleanup.live_unreconciled = true;
            return Err(self.invariant("swift_frame_live_mismatch"));
        }
        Ok(())
    }
    fn work(&mut self, amount: usize) -> Result<(), SwiftRunFailure> {
        self.phase.operation.control.work_with_wasi(
            Work::Execution,
            amount as u64,
            self.phase.operation.cursor,
            Some(self.phase.operation.wasi.usage()),
        )
    }
    fn acquire_pin(&mut self, function: u32) -> PinAttempt {
        let frame_bound = self
            .phase
            .operation
            .control
            .usage(Some(self.phase.operation.wasi.usage()))
            .reservations
            .construction
            .current;
        let mut preparation = Some(self.phase.parts.cache.prepare_operation(
            self.phase.operation.control,
            Some(&*self.phase.operation.wasi),
            self.phase.operation.cursor,
        ));
        match preparation.take().expect("occupied cache preparation slot") {
            Err(failure) => PinAttempt {
                pin: None,
                failure: Some(failure),
                nested_restore: None,
            },
            Ok(mut phase) => {
                let mut returned = Some(phase.pin(self.phase.parts.cache, function));
                let (pin, mut failure) = match returned.take().expect("occupied cache pin slot") {
                    Ok(pin) => (Some(pin), None),
                    Err(failure) => (None, Some(failure)),
                };
                #[cfg(test)]
                tests::before_cache_completion(&mut phase);
                let completion = phase.complete((preparation, returned));
                let nested_restore = if completion.is_err() {
                    Some(frame_bound)
                } else {
                    None
                };
                if let Err(completion_failure) = completion {
                    failure = failure.or(Some(completion_failure));
                }
                PinAttempt {
                    pin,
                    failure,
                    nested_restore,
                }
            }
        }
    }
    pub(crate) fn enter(&mut self, function: u32) -> Result<(), SwiftRunFailure> {
        if let Err(failure) = self.active() {
            self.terminal();
            return Err(failure);
        }
        // These real lengths survive only this synchronous entry -> rollback.
        let pre_values_len = self.phase.parts.values.len();
        let pre_controls_len = self.phase.parts.controls.len();
        let result = self.enter_inner(function);
        if result.is_err() {
            self.terminal();
            self.rollback_entry(pre_values_len, pre_controls_len);
        }
        result
    }
    fn enter_inner(&mut self, function: u32) -> Result<(), SwiftRunFailure> {
        let frame_limit = self.phase.operation.control.budget().frames.min(64);
        if self.phase.parts.frames.len() >= frame_limit
            || self.phase.parts.frames.len() == self.phase.parts.frames.capacity()
        {
            return Err(self.limit(
                SwiftRunFailureKind::FrameLimit,
                SwiftRunResource::Frames,
                "swift_frame_depth",
                self.phase.parts.frames.len().saturating_add(1),
                frame_limit,
            ));
        }
        self.work(1)?;
        let (params, result) = {
            let signature = match self.phase.parts.cache.proof().execution_signature(function) {
                Some(signature) => signature,
                None => return Err(self.invariant("swift_frame_signature")),
            };
            (signature.params.len(), signature.result.is_some())
        };
        let base = match self.phase.parts.values.len().checked_sub(params) {
            Some(base) => base,
            None => return Err(self.invariant("swift_frame_parameters")),
        };
        let operands = self
            .phase
            .parts
            .frames
            .last()
            .map_or(0, |frame| frame.operands);
        if base < operands {
            return Err(self.invariant("swift_frame_parameter_boundary"));
        }
        for index in 0..params {
            let ty = self
                .phase
                .parts
                .cache
                .proof()
                .execution_signature(function)
                .and_then(|signature| signature.params.get(index).copied());
            if ty
                != self
                    .phase
                    .parts
                    .values
                    .get(base + index)
                    .map(|value| value.ty())
            {
                return Err(self.invariant("swift_frame_parameter_type"));
            }
        }
        let PinAttempt {
            pin,
            failure,
            nested_restore,
        } = self.acquire_pin(function);
        // The actual State owns both acquired authority and restore obligation
        // before a failed completion can return out of this method.
        self.cleanup.nested_restore = nested_restore;
        if pin.is_some() {
            self.cleanup.entry = Some(EntryGuard {
                pin,
                values_debited: 0,
                controls_debited: 0,
                frame_debited: false,
            });
            self.cleanup.entry_attempted = false;
        }
        if let Some(failure) = failure {
            return Err(failure);
        }
        let (locals, end) = {
            let guard = self
                .cleanup
                .entry
                .as_ref()
                .expect("successful real pin acquisition");
            let view = self.phase.parts.cache.view(
                guard.pin.as_ref().unwrap(),
                self.phase.operation.control,
                Some(&*self.phase.operation.wasi),
            )?;
            let end = match view.instructions.len().checked_sub(1) {
                Some(end) => end,
                None => return Err(self.invariant("swift_frame_empty_function")),
            };
            (view.locals.len(), end)
        };
        let total_locals = match params.checked_add(locals) {
            Some(total) => total,
            None => return Err(self.invariant("swift_frame_locals_overflow")),
        };
        if total_locals
            > self
                .phase
                .parts
                .cache
                .proof()
                .decode_policy()
                .parameters_plus_locals()
        {
            return Err(self.limit(
                SwiftRunFailureKind::ValueLimit,
                SwiftRunResource::Values,
                "swift_frame_locals_limit",
                total_locals,
                256,
            ));
        }
        let value_capacity = self.phase.parts.values.capacity();
        if locals > value_capacity.saturating_sub(self.phase.parts.values.len()) {
            return Err(self.limit(
                SwiftRunFailureKind::ValueLimit,
                SwiftRunResource::Values,
                "swift_frame_value_capacity",
                self.phase.parts.values.len().saturating_add(locals),
                value_capacity,
            ));
        }
        let control_capacity = self.phase.parts.controls.capacity();
        if self.phase.parts.controls.len() >= control_capacity {
            return Err(self.limit(
                SwiftRunFailureKind::ControlLimit,
                SwiftRunResource::Controls,
                "swift_frame_control_capacity",
                self.phase.parts.controls.len().saturating_add(1),
                control_capacity,
            ));
        }
        // A new function has exactly one function-inclusive control. The
        // caller's own inclusive depth must also remain within the sealed512.
        if let Some(caller) = self.phase.parts.frames.last() {
            if self
                .phase
                .parts
                .controls
                .len()
                .saturating_sub(caller.controls)
                > self
                    .phase
                    .parts
                    .cache
                    .proof()
                    .decode_policy()
                    .control_depth()
            {
                return Err(self.limit(
                    SwiftRunFailureKind::ControlLimit,
                    SwiftRunResource::Controls,
                    "swift_frame_control_depth",
                    self.phase
                        .parts
                        .controls
                        .len()
                        .saturating_sub(caller.controls),
                    512,
                ));
            }
        }
        let local_work = match locals.checked_add(1) {
            Some(work) => work,
            None => return Err(self.invariant("swift_frame_work_overflow")),
        };
        self.work(local_work)?;
        for index in 0..locals {
            #[cfg(test)]
            tests::before_local(self.phase.operation.control, index);
            self.phase.operation.control.check_with_wasi(
                self.phase.operation.cursor,
                Some(self.phase.operation.wasi.usage()),
            )?;
            let ty = {
                let guard = self.cleanup.entry.as_ref().unwrap();
                let view = self.phase.parts.cache.view(
                    guard.pin.as_ref().unwrap(),
                    self.phase.operation.control,
                    Some(&*self.phase.operation.wasi),
                )?;
                let borrowed = view.locals.get(index);
                borrowed.copied()
            };
            let value = match ty {
                Some(ty) => Value::zero(ty),
                None => return Err(self.invariant("swift_frame_local_type")),
            };
            self.add_live(Live::Values)?;
            self.phase.parts.values.push(value);
            self.cleanup.entry.as_mut().unwrap().values_debited += 1;
        }
        let operands = self.phase.parts.values.len();
        let controls = self.phase.parts.controls.len();
        let function_control = Control::function_entry(end, operands, result);
        self.add_live(Live::Controls)?;
        self.phase.parts.controls.push(function_control);
        self.cleanup.entry.as_mut().unwrap().controls_debited = 1;
        self.add_live(Live::Frames)?;
        self.cleanup.entry.as_mut().unwrap().frame_debited = true;
        let mut guard = self.cleanup.entry.take().expect("actual unpublished guard");
        let pin = guard.pin.take().expect("actual acquired pin");
        self.phase.parts.frames.push(SwiftFrame {
            function,
            pc: 0,
            base,
            operands,
            controls,
            result,
            pin,
        });
        guard.values_debited = 0;
        guard.controls_debited = 0;
        guard.frame_debited = false;
        drop(guard);
        self.cleanup.entry_attempted = false;
        self.agreement()
    }
    fn add_live(&mut self, resource: Live) -> Result<(), SwiftRunFailure> {
        #[cfg(test)]
        let old = tests::before_predebit(self.phase.operation.control, resource);
        let result = self.phase.operation.control.add_live_with_wasi(
            resource,
            1,
            self.phase.operation.cursor,
            Some(self.phase.operation.wasi.usage()),
        );
        #[cfg(test)]
        tests::after_predebit(self.phase.operation.control, resource, old);
        result
    }
    fn remove_live(&mut self, resource: Live, amount: usize) -> bool {
        if self
            .phase
            .operation
            .control
            .remove_live_with_wasi(resource, amount, Some(self.phase.operation.wasi.usage()))
            .is_err()
        {
            self.cleanup.live_unreconciled = true;
            false
        } else {
            true
        }
    }
    fn release(&mut self, pin: SwiftFunctionPin, function: u32) -> Result<(), SwiftReleaseFailure> {
        #[cfg(not(test))]
        let _ = function;
        #[cfg(test)]
        tests::before_release(self.phase.parts.cache, function);
        let result = self.phase.parts.cache.release(
            pin,
            self.phase.operation.control,
            Some(&*self.phase.operation.wasi),
        );
        #[cfg(test)]
        tests::after_release(self.phase.parts.cache);
        result
    }
    fn rollback_entry(&mut self, pre_values_len: usize, pre_controls_len: usize) {
        if self.cleanup.entry.is_none() {
            return;
        }
        let values = self.phase.parts.values.len().checked_sub(pre_values_len);
        let controls = self
            .phase
            .parts
            .controls
            .len()
            .checked_sub(pre_controls_len);
        if values != Some(self.cleanup.entry.as_ref().unwrap().values_debited)
            || controls != Some(self.cleanup.entry.as_ref().unwrap().controls_debited)
        {
            self.cleanup.live_unreconciled = true;
            self.invariant("swift_frame_rollback_mismatch");
        }
        if let Some(values) = values {
            let acquired = values.min(self.cleanup.entry.as_ref().unwrap().values_debited);
            self.phase.parts.values.truncate(pre_values_len);
            if self.remove_live(Live::Values, acquired) {
                self.cleanup.entry.as_mut().unwrap().values_debited = self
                    .cleanup
                    .entry
                    .as_ref()
                    .unwrap()
                    .values_debited
                    .saturating_sub(acquired);
            }
        }
        if let Some(controls) = controls {
            let acquired = controls.min(self.cleanup.entry.as_ref().unwrap().controls_debited);
            self.phase.parts.controls.truncate(pre_controls_len);
            if self.remove_live(Live::Controls, acquired) {
                self.cleanup.entry.as_mut().unwrap().controls_debited = self
                    .cleanup
                    .entry
                    .as_ref()
                    .unwrap()
                    .controls_debited
                    .saturating_sub(acquired);
            }
        }
        if self.cleanup.entry.as_ref().unwrap().frame_debited && self.remove_live(Live::Frames, 1) {
            self.cleanup.entry.as_mut().unwrap().frame_debited = false;
        }
        self.retire_entry();
    }
    fn retire_entry(&mut self) {
        if self.cleanup.entry.is_none() || self.cleanup.entry_attempted {
            return;
        }
        self.cleanup.entry_attempted = true;
        let pin = self.cleanup.entry.as_mut().unwrap().pin.take();
        if let Some(pin) = pin {
            if let Err(rejected) = self.release(pin, u32::MAX) {
                let _failure = rejected.failure;
                self.cleanup.entry.as_mut().unwrap().pin = Some(rejected.into_pin());
            }
        }
        let guard = self.cleanup.entry.as_ref().unwrap();
        if guard.pin.is_none()
            && guard.values_debited == 0
            && guard.controls_debited == 0
            && !guard.frame_debited
        {
            self.cleanup.entry = None;
            self.cleanup.entry_attempted = false;
        }
    }
    pub(crate) fn finish(&mut self) -> Result<FrameReturn, SwiftRunFailure> {
        let result = self.finish_inner();
        if result.is_err() {
            self.terminal();
        }
        result
    }
    fn finish_inner(&mut self) -> Result<FrameReturn, SwiftRunFailure> {
        self.active()?;
        let result = match self.phase.parts.frames.last() {
            Some(frame) => frame.result,
            None => return Err(self.invariant("swift_frame_finish_empty")),
        };
        let saved = if result {
            Some(self.phase.pop_value()?)
        } else {
            None
        };
        let frame = self.phase.parts.frames.last().unwrap();
        let base = frame.base;
        let controls = frame.controls;
        let values_removed = match self.phase.parts.values.len().checked_sub(base) {
            Some(n) => n,
            None => return Err(self.invariant("swift_frame_finish_values")),
        };
        let controls_removed = match self.phase.parts.controls.len().checked_sub(controls) {
            Some(n) => n,
            None => return Err(self.invariant("swift_frame_finish_controls")),
        };
        let work = match values_removed
            .checked_add(controls_removed)
            .and_then(|n| n.checked_add(1))
        {
            Some(n) => n,
            None => return Err(self.invariant("swift_frame_finish_work")),
        };
        self.work(work)?;
        let SwiftFrame {
            function,
            pc,
            base,
            operands,
            controls,
            result,
            pin,
        } = self.phase.parts.frames.pop().expect("actual top frame");
        self.phase.parts.values.truncate(base);
        self.remove_live(Live::Values, values_removed);
        self.phase.parts.controls.truncate(controls);
        self.remove_live(Live::Controls, controls_removed);
        if let Err(rejected) = self.release(pin, function) {
            let failure = rejected.failure;
            let pin = rejected.into_pin();
            self.phase.parts.frames.push(SwiftFrame {
                function,
                pc,
                base,
                operands,
                controls,
                result,
                pin,
            });
            // This already-attempted frame is the processed suffix of this pass.
            *self.lifecycle = Lifecycle::Terminal;
            self.cleanup.disposition = FrameDisposition::Draining;
            self.cleanup.unvisited = self.phase.parts.frames.len() - 1;
            return Err(failure);
        }
        self.remove_live(Live::Frames, 1);
        if self.cleanup.live_unreconciled {
            return Err(self.invariant("swift_frame_finish_unreconciled"));
        }
        self.agreement()?;
        if self.phase.parts.frames.is_empty() {
            return Ok(FrameReturn::Outermost(saved));
        }
        if let Some(value) = saved {
            #[cfg(test)]
            let old = tests::before_result_push(self.phase.operation.control);
            let pushed = self.phase.push_value(value);
            #[cfg(test)]
            tests::after_result_push(self.phase.operation.control, old);
            pushed?;
        }
        Ok(FrameReturn::CallerContinues)
    }
    fn restore_nested(&mut self) {
        if let Some(bound) = self.cleanup.nested_restore {
            if self
                .phase
                .operation
                .control
                .end_construction_phase_with_wasi(
                    (),
                    bound,
                    Some(self.phase.operation.wasi.usage()),
                )
                .is_ok()
            {
                self.cleanup.nested_restore = None;
            }
        }
    }
    fn report(&mut self) -> FrameDrainReport {
        let first_failure = self
            .phase
            .operation
            .control
            .record_cleanup(Some(self.phase.operation.wasi.usage()));
        let usage = self
            .phase
            .operation
            .control
            .usage(Some(self.phase.operation.wasi.usage()));
        FrameDrainReport {
            retained_frames: self.phase.parts.frames.len(),
            retained_entry: self.cleanup.entry.is_some(),
            live_unreconciled: self.cleanup.live_unreconciled,
            live: usage.live,
            cache: self.phase.parts.cache.usage(),
            usage,
            bound: self.cleanup.bound,
            nested_restore: self.cleanup.nested_restore,
            first_failure,
        }
    }
    pub(crate) fn drain(&mut self) -> FrameDrainReport {
        self.terminal();
        // Retained marks a completed pass. A repeat drain does not silently
        // retry pins; only explicit cleanup-only reacquisition starts a new pass.
        if self.cleanup.disposition == FrameDisposition::Retained
            || self.cleanup.disposition == FrameDisposition::RestorePending
        {
            return self.report();
        }
        self.retire_entry();
        let live = self
            .phase
            .operation
            .control
            .usage(Some(self.phase.operation.wasi.usage()))
            .live;
        if live.values.current != self.phase.parts.values.len()
            || live.controls.current != self.phase.parts.controls.len()
            || live.frames.current
                != self.phase.parts.frames.len()
                    + usize::from(
                        self.cleanup
                            .entry
                            .as_ref()
                            .is_some_and(|guard| guard.frame_debited),
                    )
            || live.active_pins.current != self.phase.parts.cache.usage().active_pins
        {
            self.cleanup.live_unreconciled = true;
            self.invariant("swift_frame_drain_mismatch");
        }
        let values = self.phase.parts.values.len();
        self.phase.parts.values.clear();
        self.remove_live(Live::Values, values);
        let controls = self.phase.parts.controls.len();
        self.phase.parts.controls.clear();
        self.remove_live(Live::Controls, controls);
        while self.cleanup.unvisited != 0 {
            self.cleanup.unvisited -= 1;
            let index = self.cleanup.unvisited;
            let SwiftFrame {
                function,
                pc,
                base,
                operands,
                controls,
                result,
                pin,
            } = self.phase.parts.frames.remove(index);
            match self.release(pin, function) {
                Ok(()) => {
                    self.remove_live(Live::Frames, 1);
                }
                Err(rejected) => {
                    let _failure = rejected.failure;
                    let pin = rejected.into_pin();
                    self.phase.parts.frames.insert(
                        index,
                        SwiftFrame {
                            function,
                            pc,
                            base,
                            operands,
                            controls,
                            result,
                            pin,
                        },
                    );
                }
            }
        }
        self.restore_nested();
        self.cleanup.disposition = FrameDisposition::Retained;
        self.report()
    }
}
impl<'a> FrameOperationPhase<'a> {
    pub(crate) fn complete(self, storage: FrameOperationStorage<'a>) -> FrameCompletion {
        let FrameOperationPhase {
            phase,
            cleanup,
            lifecycle,
        } = self;
        let ExecutionOperationPhase {
            parts, operation, ..
        } = phase;
        let SwiftExecutionOperation { control, wasi, .. } = operation;
        let live = control.usage(Some(wasi.usage())).live;
        let clean = parts.frames.is_empty()
            && parts.values.is_empty()
            && parts.controls.is_empty()
            && cleanup.entry.is_none()
            && cleanup.unvisited == 0
            && !cleanup.entry_attempted
            && !cleanup.live_unreconciled
            && cleanup.nested_restore.is_none()
            && parts.cache.usage().active_pins == 0
            && live.values.current == 0
            && live.controls.current == 0
            && live.frames.current == 0
            && live.active_pins.current == 0;
        let mut report = FrameDrainReport {
            retained_frames: parts.frames.len(),
            retained_entry: cleanup.entry.is_some(),
            live_unreconciled: cleanup.live_unreconciled,
            live,
            cache: parts.cache.usage(),
            usage: control.usage(Some(wasi.usage())),
            bound: cleanup.bound,
            nested_restore: cleanup.nested_restore,
            first_failure: control.record_cleanup(Some(wasi.usage())),
        };
        if !clean {
            drop((parts, storage));
            *lifecycle = Lifecycle::Terminal;
            cleanup.disposition = FrameDisposition::Retained;
            return FrameCompletion::Retained(report);
        }
        let bound = match cleanup.bound {
            Some(bound) => bound,
            None => {
                drop((parts, storage));
                *lifecycle = Lifecycle::Terminal;
                cleanup.disposition = FrameDisposition::Retained;
                return FrameCompletion::Retained(report);
            }
        };
        #[cfg(test)]
        let previous = tests::outer_restore_target(bound.previous);
        #[cfg(not(test))]
        let previous = bound.previous;
        let restored = control.end_construction_phase_with_wasi(
            (parts, storage),
            previous,
            Some(wasi.usage()),
        );
        if restored.is_err() {
            *lifecycle = Lifecycle::Terminal;
            cleanup.disposition = FrameDisposition::RestorePending;
            report.usage = control.usage(Some(wasi.usage()));
            report.first_failure = control.record_cleanup(Some(wasi.usage()));
            return FrameCompletion::RestoreRejected(report);
        }
        *cleanup = FrameCleanupState::empty();
        FrameCompletion::Completed
    }
}

#[cfg(test)]
#[path = "frame_operations_tests.rs"]
mod tests;
#[cfg(test)]
pub(super) fn observed_push_capacity(capacity: usize, length: usize) -> usize {
    tests::observed_push_capacity(capacity, length)
}

/// Frozen declarations remain fully occupied; actual implementation positions
/// are fresh positive additions, never alternate aliases or subtraction.
pub(super) const PERMANENT_BYTES: usize =
    FROZEN_PERMANENT_BYTES + size_of::<ImplementationPermanentPositions>();
pub(super) const ACTIVE_BYTES: usize =
    FROZEN_ACTIVE_BYTES + size_of::<ImplementationActivePositions>();
pub(super) const FRAME_OPERATION_BYTES: Option<usize> =
    match value_operations::VALUE_OPERATION_BYTES {
        Some(nested) => ACTIVE_BYTES.checked_add(nested),
        None => None,
    };
